### 5-AMENDMENT 12: V3 ANALYSIS PLAN, B1 tau2 AIRLINE (gpt-oss) AND B2 SPREADSHEETBENCH (2026-10-15 01:30 UTC; before any V3 final rollout)

Machine-readable copy: `experiments/configs/v3_registration.json`; hash in `experiments/registry.jsonl`. **Disclosure.** V3 is post-hoc method development and a new benchmark configuration, designed after the v1 and v2 held-out results were seen (the user-directed early unblinding of erratum 36 and the reports built on it). It is its own pre-registered family, never pooled with Suites A to E and never replacing them. At the time of writing no V3 held-out (final) score exists; the reviewer has seen no V3 search-time score. The V3 searches of some arms are running or in smoke; their train-split results are search diagnostics (rule 12G). Amendments 1 to 11 and their errata stay in force except where this amendment states otherwise; where it conflicts with 5O-1 (the three-retry cap) this amendment governs for V3.

**12A. Benchmarks and sets.** B1: tau2 airline, agent, user simulator and judge `openai/aws/gpt-oss-120b` through gateway :4113, temperature 0; official split, TRAIN = the 30 `train` ids (every search step), TEST = the 20 `test` ids (the final set, T = 20, one task = one family, so C = 20 clusters). The airline test-20 has been used in Suite C, Suite E and the v2 E window; B1 is therefore labelled a development-exposed replication, reused with the reuse count printed, and carries no sealed-set claim. B2: SpreadsheetBench, agent Gemma-4-31B-it through RITS; the test set is pending the user's choice. **No B2 final rollout may run until a dated addendum (a 5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM) fixes the test set, T, the number of trials, the competitor list and the planning MDE of 12H, and is hash-registered; the addendum follows the structure of 12B to 12L unchanged and may not read any B2 outcome.**

**12B. Arms.** CapEvolve arms: HC-stock (hill-climb with the stock gate), HC-3 and AO-2.2 (later CapEvolve versions; each version is frozen as a version = code commit, spec, prompts, helpers, hashed in the registry before its final scoring, 11D/11E; only the FIRST frozen version of HC-3 and of AO-2.2 is primary, later versions secondary with their own Holm, with no credit for picking the best). Competitors: ACE, HarnessLens, Meta-Harness, WikiSkill, MIPROv2, Trace2Skill, GEPA, full-GEPA, SkillOpt, EvoSkill, whichever run on the benchmark. The competitor list J_b of a benchmark is frozen by a registry line (`experiments/configs/v3_B1_competitors.json`, hashed) BEFORE the first final rollout of that benchmark; a competitor with fewer than 3 complete seeds at the cutoff leaves the family with the reason recorded, and no arm is added afterwards. The seed (starting agent) is the shared seed artifact verified by hash; it is the baseline of every contrast.

**12C. Search data and the train-only rule.** Every search decision of every arm, CapEvolve and competitor, uses TRAIN data only: minibatches, screens, confirmation, selection, validation, early stopping, hyperparameter choices, arm configuration and the choice of the champion. A framework that needs its own validation set carves it from the 30 train ids. TEST ids, test traces and test gold are never read, scored or logged during search; the optimizer sandbox exposes no test id. No V3 design, freeze or launch decision may use any V3 or earlier held-out score (11D); a decision that used one is a deviation and is disclosed as an erratum before it is made. Search protocol (V3 brief, scratch `baselines/V3_RUN_BRIEF.md`): same seed, k = 5 trials per task with fresh trial seeds, R = 3000 counted target rollouts per search as the binding stop (a rollout is one task times one trial; other limits raised so that R binds; rollouts actually used are reported; a structurally one-pass framework reports what it used), optimizer Sonnet 5.5, 3 independent search seeds per framework. The champion of a run is the final state under the arm's own native rule at the end of its search (no matched-checkpoint truncation; R is the matched budget); a run that ends early reports its stop reason and counts.

**12D. Final scoring.** One interleaved, randomized window per benchmark (5H and 5I apply: order seed registered, order file hashed before the first rollout, hourly blocks, drift rule with block-adjusted headline if the in-window seed drifts by more than 2 SE). B1: every champion (3 CapEvolve arms x 3 seeds plus competitors x 3 seeds), the seed artifact and a 'seed-check' cell are scored on test-20 with k = 10 trials per task each, with fresh trial seeds drawn from the order seed; the window re-scores nothing from search time. Inert artifacts (tree hash equal to the seed's) follow 9D: delta 0 by construction, mapped to the seed-check cell, no separate cell. Version and scorer pins (adapter commit, harness hashes, gateway settings) are recorded in the order file and hashed. Held-out results stay blind (files hash-registered, no read) until the coordinator declares the V3 freeze after all V3 searches and the window have ended, or until an explicit recorded user decision to unblind (as erratum 36); no held-out number goes to a runner.

**12E. Primary family (confirmatory), per benchmark, one Holm.** Each CapEvolve arm c in {HC-stock, HC-3, AO-2.2} against (i) the seed and (ii) each frozen competitor j in J_b: m = 3 x (1 + |J_b|) rows (up to 33 on B1). Test = artifact-level cluster-robust t (task = cluster, paired by task, CR1, t with C - 1 df) of 5M-2; the seeds of the competitor are averaged per task as in the Suite A to E analyses. Holm-adjusted p is the verdict. Companion, not a claim basis: Holm p within the sub-family 'vs seed' (m = 3) and within 'vs competitors', reported beside. A row whose arm is fully inert reads 'returned the seed' (9D(iv)). Secondary families (own Holm each): later versions; CapEvolve arm vs CapEvolve arm (descriptive, no Holm claim: HC-stock vs HC-3 vs AO-2.2); competitor vs seed.

**12F. Intervals.** Every primary row reports both: artifact-level CI (CR1 t(C - 1) on the task-level deltas of the S frozen champions; the basis of the test and of Holm) and pipeline-level CI (t(S - 1) with SE^2 = SE_task^2 + s_seed^2 / S, 5M-2). Any sentence about 'the method' or 'the optimizer' is made only if the pipeline-level interval excludes 0; otherwise it is limited to the frozen artifacts. Per-seed deltas are shown for every row. SD, SE and CI vocabulary as in section 4. No equivalence claim on B1 (T = 20: a TOST at margin 0.10 has design power of about one half at zero true difference); non-significant rows read 'inconclusive' with the TOST design power printed.

**12G. Search-time comparisons are descriptive.** Search-time results (train scores, candidate curves, promotions per search, gate behaviour, rollouts used, wall time, optimizer cost) of the CapEvolve arms against each other and against competitors are descriptive: they are selected-on, taken at different times and loads, and never enter a test, a Holm family or a headline. The only comparisons offered as evidence are the final-window ones of 12E.

**12H. Planning MDE (B1; conditional on the 3 frozen champions, 80% power, uncorrected unless stated; within-task variance per trial 0.1236 from the Suite C gpt-oss airline probe, T = 20, k = 10 for arms and seed, S = 3, tau 0.15; `analysis/scripts/mde_observed.py`).** Conditional: 0.122 for one contrast, 0.144 at Holm m = 3, 0.183 at m = 24, 0.188 at m = 33; noise-only floor (tau = 0) 0.071 at m = 1 and 0.110 at m = 33. Pipeline-level (t, 2 df): 0.24 at m = 1, 0.39 at m = 3, and above 1.0 at m = 24 and m = 33, i.e. a pipeline-level claim is out of reach under the full family; it is available only for the single-contrast or sub-family views. Differences below the conditional MDE of the row's m are reported as inconclusive. These are planning values, not power guarantees. The B2 MDE is computed in its addendum from the B2 observed variance.

**12I. Errors and missingness (zero-error regime; supersedes 5O-1 for V3).** (1) Every rollout that ends in an LLM, infrastructure or gateway error (including timeouts, the gateway-side failures and the `###STOP###` leak artifact) is retried with a fresh trial seed until it succeeds; no such rollout is ever scored 0 or silently dropped, and every retry is logged (retry count per cell is reported as in 5O-3). A cell that cannot succeed after 20 attempts or 24 hours is treated as missing and flagged; it enters the sensitivity bounds of section 6 and the primary result is reported both ways if the conclusion differs. (2) Rule 5a: a tool-result consistency failure of a candidate's `tools.py` (classified exactly as in the V3 brief: termination INFRASTRUCTURE_ERROR, error type ValueError, message starting `Tool call:` and containing `Returned:` and `Expected:`) is a CANDIDATE fault: reward 0, counted toward R in search and as a trial in the final window, NOT retried, the same for every arm and for the seed. Primary analysis keeps the zeros (intention to treat); sensitivity treats them as missing; the rate of candidate faults per arm is reported with Wilson intervals next to the seed's (5O-4 style) and flagged when clearly higher. (3) Hangs past 3 h: one retry, then missing (5O-2). (4) Stress sensitivity: first-attempt infrastructure errors scored 0 (`results_long_first_attempt_infra_zero.csv`). (5) A framework whose internals swallow errors is patched before its search; the patch and its hash are in the run record. (6) Empty-output rate (5K) is reported for every arm.

**12J. Defects and voided decisions.** Before a champions file is registered, the reviewer greps the run logs of the arm for refused commits, crash-relaunch loops and swallowed-error counters; a champion whose accept was voided is handled as in erratum 43 and disclosed. Any exposure of evaluator events of held-out data in an optimizer sandbox is disclosed as in erratum 16. The pooled-mode commit defect (PR #861) is fixed in the code version used by V3; each CapEvolve arm's code commit is registered before its first search rollout.

**12K. Cost and reporting.** Per arm and seed: optimizer tokens and priced USD (Sonnet 5.5 at the proxy list price of `protocol/pricing.json`; gpt-oss price unknown, reported in tokens, never 0), counted search rollouts used, target tokens and wall time per rollout, ledger tags `tag:V3-B1-<framework>-s<k>:<role>` (target, user, judge, optimizer; the :4113 ledger is the source of truth), infrastructure and candidate-fault rates, promotions per search, inert fraction, selection-to-final shrinkage over non-inert seeds (5P-2, selection split = train), and cost per point (9D(vi)). Every table cell states n tasks, n families, n seeds and n trials per task.

**12L. Cross-benchmark rule and freeze.** A claim spanning B1 and B2 about one configuration is made only if the row passes in both benchmarks' own families (9B); no pooling and no cross-benchmark p-value. The registry holds, before the first final rollout of a benchmark: this plan (hash), `v3_registration.json`, the competitor list, the version freeze lines of each CapEvolve arm, the champions files (after the grep of 12J), the artifact manifest, the comparison-family file, the order file and the scorer pins. The comparison-family file lists exactly the rows of 12E. The reviewer is the sole registry owner and acks each window before launch.

Each window is one interleaved randomized window with the rules of 12D: order file hashed before the first rollout, 5I drift rule on its in-window seed, test-20 at k = 10, inert artifacts mapped to the seed-check cell (9D). (2) *Cells of each window.* The late competitor's three champions; the seed artifact at n = 20; the seed-check at k = 10; and the 9 CapEvolve champions (HC-stock, HC-3, AO-2.2, three seeds each, the frozen artifacts of the main window, same tree hashes) re-scored at k = 10. (3) *Family.* Each window is a SECONDARY family with its own Holm: LC-ACE and LC-HL, rows = each of the three CapEvolve arms against the late competitor (m = 3), paired by task, artifact-level CR1 t and pipeline-level t(S - 1) as in 12F; the competitor against the seed in the same window is shown descriptively. Contrasts use only the cells of the window itself (11F): no contrast between a cell of a follow-up window and a cell of the main window or of the other follow-up window is computed, and the late competitor never enters P2 of the main window, whose Holm families (m = 3 and m = 8) are unchanged. The CapEvolve re-scores of each follow-up window are a measure of window-to-window noise, reported beside the main-window means as a descriptive check (the same artifacts, different time blocks). (4) *Planning.* Jobs per window: late competitor 3 x 200, seed 400, seed-check 200, CapEvolve 9 x 200 = 3,000. The planning MDE for m = 3 is that of 12H (conditional 0.139 at tau 0.15 with the seed at n = 20). (5) *Registry.* The reviewer registers the late competitor's champions (after the 12J grep of its run logs for swallowed errors and refused commits), the artifact manifest, the comparison-family file, the order and the scorer pins before launch and acks each window; results hashes are registered before any analysis; the held-out results of a follow-up window stay blind until the coordinator's freeze declaration for that window. (6) *Disclosure.* The main-window V3 B1 results had been analysed and seen when this addendum was written; the follow-up design (cells, family, Holm) is fixed by the rules above and does not depend on any ACE or HarnessLens score, none of which exists.

About 3,400 jobs. (3) *Families,* each with its own Holm, secondary: LC-ACE (m = 3: each CapEvolve arm vs ACE) and LC-HL (m = 3: each CapEvolve arm vs hl-v0), paired by task, artifact-level CR1 t. **5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 4: V4B1 decision rule for replacing V3 HC-stock by HC-v4 (2026-10-18 04:25 UTC; registered before any HC-v4 held-out or train-30 re-score exists; requested by b1-rerun, relayed rule text below).** *Window V4B1* (to be built by final-eval-eng; its order, artifacts and scorer pins come to the reviewer for hashing and an ack before launch, as for every window): test-20 at k = 10; cells: HC-v4 x 3 seeds (`runs/V4/B1/hcv4/s{0,1,2}/final`), V3 HC-stock x 3 seeds re-scored frozen in the same window (`runs/V3/B1/hcstock/s{0,1,2}/final`, same tree hashes as the main window), the seed at n = 20 and the seed-check at k = 10. A separate DESCRIPTIVE block scores the train-30 ids at k = 5 for HC-v4 x 3 and HC-stock x 3. *Decision rule (verbatim from b1-rerun, to be applied mechanically):* HC-v4 replaces V3 HC-stock in the paper only if (i) the mean of HC-v4 over its 3 search seeds on test-20 is greater than or equal to the mean of V3 HC-stock over its 3 search seeds on test-20 IN THE SAME WINDOW, AND (ii) the mean of HC-v4 on train-30 (k = 5 re-score) is greater than or equal to the mean of HC-stock on train-30. Otherwise V3 HC-stock stays the headline and HC-v4 is reported as an additional arm ('V4 features'), honestly. Also reported descriptively: agent tokens and USD per rollout for HC-v4 vs HC-stock, and the search cost of both from the :4113 ledger. *Reviewer's annotations, fixed now so the rule is not reinterpreted after the numbers:* (a) The rule is a deterministic decision on point estimates; it is not a hypothesis test and carries no Holm family or p-value. The window shows the per-seed means, the difference of the 3-seed means with its artifact-level CR1 CI (task clusters, paired by task) and the pipeline-level t(2) interval, but the rule uses the point comparison only; a replacement that passes the rule with a difference inside the CI is described as 'not distinguishable', not as an improvement. (b) A tie counts as 'greater than or equal'; means are the arithmetic mean of the three per-seed means as printed in the analysis table (three decimals). (c) The V3 main-window result of HC-stock (erratum 9, P1 and P2 rows) and the LATE and CHAIN results are NOT altered by this rule: they stay in the paper as registered. If HC-v4 replaces HC-stock as the headline, the paper reports both and states the selection (the rule uses the test-20 and the train-30, so the HC-v4 test mean is selection-conditioned on having passed the rule and is not an unbiased estimate of its generalization). (d) Window-to-window noise: HC-stock is re-scored in the V4B1 window precisely so that the comparison is within one window; no cross-window comparison with the main-window HC-stock mean decides the rule (the AO-2.2 re-score moved by 0.06 between windows, erratum 14). (e) If the in-window seed drift check of V4B1 flags (5I), the block-adjusted means are used in the rule for both arms, and the unadjusted comparison is reported beside; if the two disagree on the outcome of the rule, both are reported and the block-adjusted one decides. (f) HC-v4 was designed and searched after the main V3 B1 results were seen (disclosure); it enters no P1, P2, LC or CHAIN family. (g) The rule is registered before the HC-v4 seeds have ended; the reviewer has seen no HC-v4 score. Not verified by the reviewer: that no hcv4 test or train-30 re-score exists (stated by b1-rerun).

The decision rule of addendum 4 therefore cannot be applied (it needs the 3-seed means and the in-window HC-stock); HC-stock stays the V3 headline as registered, and this window is descriptive and adds one arm, 'HC-v4 (best of 3 by train)'. *Selection, disclosed.* The champion is HC-v4 seed 2, candidate `cand_0002`, chosen as the one of the three HC-v4 search seeds with the highest search-time train score (0.787; the other two final champions have 0.733 and 0.760 on the same measure, from `candidates.jsonl` of the three runs: s0 `cand_0015` 0.733, s1 `cand_0014` 0.760). The choice used train data only and no held-out score of HC-v4; but it is a best-of-3 selection, so the held-out score of this champion is selection-conditioned and is an optimistic estimate of the average HC-v4 seed. *Registered:* `order_V4B1S2.jsonl` `90a03cd9...` (801 lines = 800 jobs + meta: hcv4 s2 x 200 = 20 test tasks x k = 10, seed n = 20 = 400, seed-check 200; adapter `9c797d8a...`; same machinery and scorer as V3B1), `artifacts_V4B1S2.json` `58ca76bb...`, `champions_V4B1S2.json` `1953eea3...`, and the hcv4 s2 `run_summary.json` and `candidates.jsonl`. The order and manifest live in the paper-repo checkout `mlsys2027-cap-evolve-paper` (commit `cf5be1b`), not on the campaign branch; the registry records their path. *Verified from disk:* hashes equal b1-rerun's message; the order pins adapter `9c797d8a...`, test-20, k = 10, seed n = 20; the frozen artifact files equal the manifest; the artifact's tree hash is new (it equals neither the seed nor any artifact scored in the V3 B1 main, CHAIN or REJ windows); hcv4 s2's accepted candidate list has exactly one entry, `cand_0002`, equal to the champion in `run_summary.json`. *Analysis rules fixed now:* descriptive only; no P1, P2, LC or Holm family; (1) HC-v4 s2 minus the in-window seed (n = 20) with the artifact-level CR1 CI and the paired p, the headline; (2) the cross-window comparison with the V3 B1 main-window HC-stock (delta +0.131 over a main-window seed of 0.498; the V3 HC-stock champions, not HC-v4, were selected by the search's own rule) is expressed as 'delta against the window's own seed', never as a difference of raw means, and carries the window-shift caveat (the AO-2.2 re-score moved by 0.06 between windows, erratum 14) and the best-of-3 caveat; (3) the drift rule of 5I applies to the in-window seed; (4) one champion means no pipeline-level interval and no statement about the method. **5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 23: V4B1S2 analysed (2026-10-19 17:13 UTC; release by the coordinator, user-approved test).** Disclosure: b1-rerun computed and pushed descriptive numbers (`results/V4_B1_rerun/test_s2.md`, commit `ae5df15`) before the coordinator's release word and before the reviewer read the results; the reviewer's analysis is independent and follows the rules of erratum 21 (script `analysis/scripts/v4b1s2_analysis.py`, registered after its first run, one caption note added after the first run). Agreement with b1-rerun: their +0.080 [0.017, 0.143], p 0.031, 12 better / 2 worse / 6 tied reproduce exactly under their methods (task percentile bootstrap, exact sign-flip); the registered CR1 interval [0.011, 0.149] is wider than their percentile interval [0.017, 0.143] on both sides, and the registered p is 0.026; both exclude 0; no correction of their file is required beyond adding the CR1 interval. Cross-window, as deltas over each window's own seed: V3 HC-stock main-window 3-seed mean +0.131 [0.013, 0.248] (seeds +0.118, +0.148, +0.128) against HC-v4 s2 +0.080 [0.011, 0.149]; the HC-v4 delta is smaller although it is the best of 3 by train; the comparison is not a test, windows differ (up to 0.06), and no claim of superiority in either direction is made. HC-stock stays the V3 headline; HC-v4 is an additional arm, one champion, no pipeline-level interval.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 5: ablation and native-Claude-Code held-out window design (2026-10-21 00:12 UTC; before any ablation or NC artifact is scored; request of ablation-lead).** *Disclosure.* The ablation (12 arms) and the native Claude Code arms (NC-1SHOT, NC-LOOP, NC-SKILLS) were designed after the main V3 B1 results were seen (and the 12 ablation searches had ended); they enter no registered V3 family (P1, P2, LC, CHAIN, REJ, V4). *Window.* Same as the V3 B1 main window: tau2 airline official test-20, k = 10 trials per task per artifact, agent, user simulator and judge `openai/aws/gpt-oss-120b` via :4113, adapter `9c797d8a...`, one interleaved randomized window (5H, 5I), order hashed before the first rollout. *Cells.* The 12 ablation champions (1 search seed each): CAP-P `cand_0010`, CAP-TD `cand_0005`, CAP-TC `cand_0004`, HIST-NONE `cand_0013`, HIST-STATE `cand_0001`, INFO-REWARD `cand_0007`, INFO-EVAL `cand_0005`, INFO-FAIL `cand_0018`, GATE-K0.5 `cand_0007`, GATE-K2 `cand_0014`, GATE-NONE `cand_0019`, GATE-DISJOINT `cand_0017`; the 3 NC champions once frozen; the seed at n = 20 trials per task; the seed-check at k = 10; and, as the reviewer's addition so that the comparison asked for is made inside one window, the V3 B1 HC-stock seed-0 champion (`hcstock` s0, `cand_0012`, the same frozen artifact as in the main window) re-scored at k = 10 as the in-window reference. Inert champions (tree hash equal to the seed's) are mapped to the seed-check cell with delta 0, as in 9D. *Window structure (reviewer's preference).* Option A, one window of all 15 + reference, if the three NC searches have ended with frozen champions within about 36 hours of this record; then one seed control serves all. If not, option B: the 12 ablation champions + the HC-stock reference now, and the NC arms in a second window with its own seed, seed-check and HC-stock reference (11F: no contrast between windows; every arm is a delta over its own window's seed, and the HC-stock reference gives the same-window comparison in both). *Verified from disk (counts and ids only; no held-out score exists):* for each of the 12 ablation runs, the champion in `run_summary.json` equals the last accepted candidate in `candidates.jsonl` and equals the id named by ablation-lead; all 12 used 3,000 counted search rollouts; the final policy and tools of the 12 are pairwise distinct and none equals the seed. HIST-STATE's champion is its first and only accepted candidate (`cand_0001`); GATE-NONE accepted 19 of its candidates. The run summaries are hash-registered. Not yet verified: the 12J grep of the ablation and NC run logs for refused commits, crash loops and swallowed errors (to be done before the champions file is registered), the NC artifacts and their search budget, and the scorer pins. *Analysis rules fixed now.* Descriptive. Each arm: delta over the in-window seed with the artifact-level CR1 CI and the paired p; the comparison with the in-window HC-stock s0 is the difference of the two deltas on the same tasks (task-paired CR1). One search seed per arm: no pipeline-level interval, no statement about the method or about a component's general importance; an arm's difference to HC-stock s0 is a statement about two artifacts. A companion Holm adjustment over the 15 arm-versus-seed rows is reported beside the raw p and decides nothing. The 5I seed drift rule applies. The seed trials are 20 per task as in the main window (the seed-check 10). Results stay blind until the coordinator's release for this window.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 8: ABL-K2-MAIN, the three GATE-K2 champions against the three HC-stock (k = 1) champions (2026-10-21 12:04 UTC; registered before the GATE-K2 seeds 1 and 2 have ended and before any ABL-K2-MAIN score exists; request of team-lead).** *Disclosure (required).* The gate setting k = 2 (a paired gate that accepts only a candidate whose mean paired delta exceeds 2 SE) was chosen for this confirmation AFTER the held-out result of GATE-K2 seed 0 had been seen in ABL-W1 (delta +0.145 over the in-window seed, raw p 0.043; block-adjusted +0.141, p 0.051; erratum 29), as one of 12 exploratory ablation arms. That choice is a selection among 12 arms on a held-out score; the held-out estimate of GATE-K2 seed 0 is therefore selection-conditioned and optimistic. Seeds 1 and 2 are fresh searches (not yet ended at the time of this record); seed 0 is the same frozen artifact that ABL-W1 scored (`runs/ABL/GATE-K2/s0/final`, `cand_0014`) and is re-scored. *Window.* One interleaved randomized window with the V3 B1 main-window protocol (test-20, k = 10 per champion, adapter `9c797d8a...`, gpt-oss-120b via :4113, order hashed before the first rollout, 5H and 5I). *Cells.* The 3 GATE-K2 champions (s0, s1, s2); the 3 HC-stock (k = 1 gate) champions of the V3 B1 main window (seeds 0, 1, 2; the same frozen artifacts, same tree hashes as in the main window); the seed at n = 20 trials per task; the seed-check at k = 10. Inert champions (tree hash equal to the seed's) are mapped to the seed-check cell with delta 0 (9D). *Families (fixed now).* (1) PRIMARY, m = 1: GATE-K2 versus the seed (mean of the three seeds' task-level scores minus the seed), artifact-level CR1 t(19) CI as the test and the pipeline-level t(S - 1 = 2) interval beside it (5M-2); the Holm denominator is 1, so the raw p is the adjusted p. (2) SECONDARY, no Holm, no adjustment: GATE-K2 versus HC-stock k = 1, paired by task, 3 seeds against 3 seeds, with the same two intervals. (3) The comparator rows for the paper: K2 vs seed (primary) and K2 vs HC-stock k = 1 (secondary). (4) SELECTION-FREE SENSITIVITY: the primary and secondary contrasts are also computed on seeds 1 and 2 only (two fresh searches, no selection on their held-out result), with the artifact-level CI and the pipeline-level interval (t with 1 df, which is very wide; reported for completeness and labelled as such), beside the three-seed estimate; if the two-seed estimate and the three-seed estimate disagree in sign or in the exclusion of 0, both are shown and neither is called the result. (5) DESCRIPTIVE, no test: the per-seed deltas, the GATE-K2 s0 re-score against its ABL-W1 delta (+0.145 over a seed of 0.485), the seed-check minus seed. *Checks and analysis.* The 5I drift rule applies to the in-window seed (block-adjusted deltas beside the plain ones when it flags; if the two disagree on the exclusion of 0 for the primary contrast, both are reported and the block-adjusted one is the headline); the seed-check minus seed is reported as the empirical null of window noise. The analysis is PRE-RELEASED: the coordinator releases the unblinding as soon as the window has ended and the reviewer has registered the results hashes; the reviewer then runs it at once. ablation-lead and other runners must compute and push no number from this window before that analysis. *Before the window:* the GATE-K2 seed 1 and 2 searches end with frozen champions; the reviewer runs the 12J grep on their run logs; the order, artifact manifest, champions file and scorer pins come to the reviewer for hashing and an ack; the search budget of seeds 1 and 2 (3,000 counted rollouts at k = 5 as in ABL-W1) is confirmed. The design also states what it cannot show: three seeds per arm, one benchmark, a gate setting chosen after a held-out look, so even a clear pipeline-level result is a statement about this configuration on this benchmark.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 9: four GATE-K2 seeds in ABL-K2-MAIN (amends addendum 8; 2026-10-22 07:50 UTC; user decision of 2026-10-22 about 07:25 UTC relayed by team-lead; registered before any held-out score of GATE-K2 seed 1, 2 or 3 exists).** (1) *Change.* GATE-K2 seed 3 is added (search launching at the time of this record). ABL-K2-MAIN now has 4 GATE-K2 champions (s0 to s3), the 3 HC-stock k = 1 champions of the V3 B1 main window, the seed at n = 20 trials per task and the seed-check at k = 10, every champion at k = 10 on test-20: 3,400 jobs (7 champions x 200 + 400 + 200 + any reference cell the reviewer adds on order review). (2) *Primary, m = 1:* GATE-K2 (4 seeds) versus the in-window seed, artifact-level CR1 t(19) CI as the test, the pipeline-level interval is now t with S - 1 = 3 df. Secondary (no Holm): GATE-K2 versus HC-stock k = 1, paired by task, 4 seeds against 3 seeds, with both intervals. (3) *Selection-free sensitivity:* seeds 1 to 3 only (all fresh searches), same contrasts, pipeline-level t with 2 df; the rule of addendum 8 on disagreement between the full and the selection-free estimate stands. (4) *All four seeds are reported whatever they score. No seed may be dropped, replaced or re-run after its held-out score exists, and a seed whose search stalls or fails enters the window with its champion as it stands (the final state of the search under the native rule, possibly the seed artifact, in which case it is inert and maps to the seed-check cell with delta 0 under 9D(iv)).* (5) *Disclosure of the reason for the change (required):* the decision to add seed 3 was taken after seeing the SEARCH-split result of seed 2 (best search-split score 0.733 against 0.913 and 0.927 for the other two seeds, as reported by team-lead); no held-out score of any GATE-K2 seed except seed 0's ABL-W1 result (disclosed in addendum 8) was seen. The added seed gives an extra draw from the same search distribution; because it was added after a weak search was seen, it could be read as an attempt to dilute that weak seed, and the rule (4) is the safeguard: seed 2 is NOT replaced and enters the primary analysis whatever its search score. The search-split scores of the four seeds are reported beside the held-out scores. (6) All other provisions of addendum 8 (window protocol, drift rule, advance release of the analysis on the results hashes, no runner computing a number first, 12J grep and the order review before launch) apply unchanged.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 35: proposer-template confound of the ablation arms and of GATE-K2 versus HC-stock (disclosure, in the scope of addenda 8 and 9; 2026-10-22 17:55 UTC; before any ABL-K2-MAIN score exists; found by an audit reported by team-lead).** *Fact (verified by the reviewer from the run directories, hashes of `.capevolve/project/optimizer/INSTRUCTIONS.md` only).* The V3 HC-stock runs (s0 to s2) use the proposer instructions template with sha256 prefix `a6370889` (reported wording: 'ship several real, safe, verified fixes'; code head `a7a83846f5`, worktree `wt-v3run`). The twelve ABL-W1 ablation arms, all four GATE-K2 seeds (s0 to s3), and also V3 HC-3 (s0 to s2) and AO-2.2 (s0 to s2) use the template `42761766` (reported wording: 'ship the edit with the largest expected reward gain'; the ABL build record names code `8079e39ae`, worktree `wt-abl`); only CAP-P (`cfe3b8ed`), CAP-TC (`683337fd`) and CAP-TD (`1520e44f`) differ from it, as a surface-restriction arm would; the HC-v4 seeds also use `42761766`. Team-lead reports that 14 commits separate the two code versions, among them changes to `proposer.py`, `policy.py`, the conductor and `act`, and that the 'byte-identical stock path' statement of the ablation design document is false for the prompt; the reviewer did not diff the code. Team-lead also reports a much higher optimizer spend of the ablation arms (about USD 40 to 157 against about 9, gateway basis), consistent with the template (about 11 times more output tokens); not verified by the reviewer. *Consequences, binding on the write-ups:* (1) The SECONDARY contrast of addendum 8/9, GATE-K2 versus HC-stock k = 1, confounds the gate setting k with the proposer template and the code version; it is labelled 'K2 + template/code version vs HC-stock', never as a gate effect. (2) Every 'ablation arm minus base' contrast of ABL-W1 confounds the switch under test with the template; there is no in-window arm that has the template and the stock gate at k = 1, so the ABL-W1 and ABL-W2 tables cannot attribute any difference to a component. This applies to the comparisons with HC-stock-s0-ref as well (an artifact searched under the older template). (3) The PRIMARY contrast of addendum 8 (GATE-K2 versus the seed) is unaffected: it compares the champions with the seed artifact, whatever process produced them. It also cannot be read as 'K2 helps': the V3 HC-3 and AO-2.2 searches, which share the template, are in the main window; a within-template control for the gate (an arm with the template and k = 1, i.e. the stock gate) is not part of ABL-K2-MAIN. (4) The statements about cost in the paper must use one basis and carry the template difference. The paper must not describe the ablation arms as 'stock path with one switch changed'.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 36: disclosure note for the V3 results on the proposer-template confound (2026-10-22 17:56 UTC; approved by team-lead; no registered number changes).** Erratum 35 established, from the hashes of the run directories, that the V3 HC-stock searches ran with the proposer instructions template `a6370889...` (code head `a7a83846f5`) while the V3 HC-3 and AO-2.2 searches ran with the template `42761766...`. Therefore, in the V3 B1 write-up: (1) the descriptive CapEvolve-arm versus CapEvolve-arm contrasts (HC-stock against HC-3 and against AO-2.2; they are descriptive under 12G, with no Holm claim) confound the method, the gate and the code version with the proposer template, and must say so; the differences between HC-stock and the other two arms (the main window, the LATE window, the CHAIN and REJ windows' references) are not evidence about the method alone. (2) The registered V3 confirmatory rows are unchanged and unaffected: P1 (each CapEvolve arm against the seed), P2 and the LC families compare each arm's artifact with the seed or with a competitor's artifact, whatever process produced it. (3) The in-V3 reading 'HC-stock is the strongest CapEvolve arm' is therefore a statement about the HC-stock configuration as run (older template and code head), not about the stock gate versus the other gates; the V4 and ablation windows' template disclosure of erratum 35 applies in addition. (4) The disclosure is added by this erratum only; the result tables and the numbers of errata 9, 14, 18, 20 and 29 are not altered.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 10: no template-control arm; ABL-K2-MAIN stays as registered (2026-10-22 18:06 UTC; user decision relayed by team-lead; before any ABL-K2-MAIN score exists).** The user decided not to run a control arm with the new proposer template and the stock gate (k = 1). ABL-K2-MAIN stays exactly as registered in addenda 8 and 9: 4 GATE-K2 champions (s0 to s3), the 3 HC-stock k = 1 champions of the main window, the seed at n = 20 and the seed-check at k = 10, every champion at k = 10 on test-20. Consequence for the wording, binding on every write-up (erratum 35): the secondary contrast is called 'K2 plus the template and code version versus HC-stock', and no sentence attributes any difference between the K2 champions and the HC-stock champions to the gate setting. The within-template effect of the gate is not identified by this design. The V3 disclosure note of erratum 36 is approved and recorded. The order of ABL-K2-MAIN is no longer held: it is to be sent to the reviewer when GATE-K2 seeds 2 and 3 have ended with frozen champions, after the 12J grep, for hashing and an ack.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 37: optimizer turn-cap truncation in the GATE-K2 searches (disclosure, in the scope of addenda 8 to 10; 2026-10-22 18:56 UTC; before any ABL-K2-MAIN score exists; measured by ablation-lead from search logs, reported by team-lead).** The optimizer sessions of the four GATE-K2 searches run with `optimizer_max_turns` 80. Count of sessions whose last assistant `stop_reason` is not `end_turn` (cut by the turn cap), from the Claude Code transcripts under each run's `claude_cfg`: seed 0, 0 of 19; seed 1, 0 of 19; seed 2, 17 of 19 (mean 154 assistant messages per session); seed 3, 2 of 13 sessions so far (the search had not ended at the time of the count). The reviewer recounted the same quantity independently from the transcripts and obtained the same four pairs of numbers. The measurement uses search logs only (no test data). *Effect on the registered rules:* none. The stall of seed 2's search (best search-split score 0.733, addendum 9) coincides with truncation in most of its steps, so seed 2's weaker search outcome has a mechanical correlate; it is a property of the search outcome and is NOT a reason to drop, replace or re-run the seed: addendum 9 (4) stands, seed 2 stays in the primary analysis. *Reporting:* the per-seed truncation counts (cut sessions / sessions) are printed beside the per-seed held-out deltas and the search-split scores in every ABL-K2-MAIN table and in the write-up; seed 3's final count is taken when its search ends and is added by the reviewer. The reading is descriptive: with four seeds and a single mechanism observed, the table does not show that truncation explains any held-out difference. The comparable truncation rates for the HC-stock searches (older template, code and turn settings) have not been measured and are not compared.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 11: ABL-V2 design, tau2 ablations v2 (2026-10-22 20:25 UTC; registered before any ABL-V2 search launches and before any ABL-V2 score exists; request of ablation-lead).** *Files registered by hash (as submitted, from `campaign-scratch/scripts/ABL2/`):* `WINDOW_DESIGN.md` `ad077569b8f81eac...`, `DESIGN.md` `dbabf18c28210c09...`, `SURFACE_PARAGRAPHS.txt` `0dd33caa2f0ca23a...`, `build_abl2.py` `316601f19c80...`, `finalize_abl2.py` `985c48017f38...`, `launch_abl2.sh` `666275eb5fe6...`. Note: `DESIGN.md` was edited at 20:23 UTC, after the reviewer first read it (first read `670f9c97...`, 7,231 bytes; registered version `dbabf18c...`, 8,072 bytes); the additions are two amendments (the lead's decision on the tools-lint option (a) with the two flagged K2 s0 docstrings, and an A2a smoke result: 0 voids, 0 optimizer errors, docstring edits and 1 to 2 new tools per candidate); the registered version is the current one. *Design, in the reviewer's words (details in the files):* 6 arms x 2 seeds (s0, s1), each arm one change from the K2 configuration (GATE-K2, gate k = 2, 3,000 counted rollouts, k = 5, 30 train tasks, optimizer 80 turns, template `42761766`): A1a naive Claude Code (one short prompt, one session, no /goal), A1b Claude Code with the CapEvolve INSTRUCTIONS and the two capability skills, A2a tools-only (surface paragraph appended; policy locked; lint), A2b policy-only (surface paragraph; tools locked; anti-overfit lint), A3a trace-and-score only, A3b evaluation-block only. Window ABL-V2 after all 12 searches have ended: 12 arm champions, the 4 K2 champions (GATE-K2 s0 to s3, same-window reference), the seed at n = 20 and the seed-check at k = 10, test-20, k = 10 per champion, adapter `9c797d8a...`: 3,800 jobs. *Rulings on the five points:* (1) *2-seed pooled contrast per arm.* Print, per arm: (a) the per-seed contrasts, each arm champion minus the K2 champion of the same seed index, task-paired, with the artifact-level CR1 CI and raw p; (b) the pooled difference, arm (mean of its two seeds) minus K2 (mean of the matching two K2 seeds, s0 and s1 only), task-paired, with the artifact-level CR1 CI and raw p; (c) the same pooled difference against all four K2 seeds as a sensitivity (the K2 s2 and s3 searches differ in truncation, erratum 37); (d) NO pipeline-level interval as a claim: with 2 seeds per arm the pipeline-level t has 1 df (planning MDE about 0.70) and is printed only as a labelled descriptor. Planning MDEs (conditional, 80% power, within-variance 0.1236, tau 0.15, T = 20, k = 10, uncorrected): 0.158 for a single champion against a single champion, 0.132 for the 2-seed pooled arm against the matching 2 K2 seeds, 0.117 against 4 K2 seeds; differences below these are inconclusive, and ten contrasts in a family mean one or two raw p-values under 0.05 are expected by chance. (2) *K2 s3* is included as a reference cell and is paired with nothing; it enters only sensitivity (c). (3) *Champion rule:* the last accepted candidate, as for K2, is the scored artifact; the best-validation candidate is logged in `run_summary.json` and reported descriptively, never scored; for the NC arms (A1a, A1b) the scored artifact is the `/work/champion` tree, with the session's stated pick logged beside it; a champion whose tree equals the seed's is inert (9D). (4) *A2a stricter than K2 on docstrings:* accepted and disclosed as the lead decided: K2 s0's champion has 2 rule-style docstring findings (`book_reservation`, `update_reservation_flights`) under the A2a lint, so tools-only is stricter than K2; A2a therefore differs from K2 in lint as well as in surface, and the A2a-versus-K2 contrast is a contrast of the two together. The lint rejections are counted per arm. (5) *Turn-cap truncation:* all v2 arms keep 80 turns; the truncation count (last `stop_reason` not `end_turn`, cut sessions over sessions) is audited for every run, arms and K2, by one method, and printed beside the per-seed contrasts (erratum 37 applies: a seed with heavy truncation stays in). *Confounds that remain by design and must be printed:* the arms were designed after the K2 and V3 B1 results were seen; two seeds per arm; the contrast of every arm with K2 is a contrast of one change (the template is shared with K2, so the template confound of erratum 35 does not enter an arm-versus-K2 comparison, but it does enter any comparison with HC-stock); A2a and A2b add a surface paragraph to the template, A3a and A3b keep it byte-identical, the NC arms have no CapEvolve loop, gate or ledger and differ in more than one way from K2 (A1a, A1b vs K2 compares a different optimizer protocol, not a component). *Families:* exploratory; raw p, no family claim, no Holm decision. *Checks before results are registered:* the 12J grep of all 12 runs (tracebacks, refused accepts, swallowed errors); the isolation grep of the NC transcripts (sealed test ids, gold fields, host paths); the surface and lint audit of every champion; the feedback-content grep of the A3a and A3b working directories at an accepted step. The reviewer does not re-derive the sandbox claims. *Process:* champions, order, artifact manifest and scorer pins come to the reviewer for hashing and an ack before the window; the 5I drift rule applies; the analysis is pre-released only if the coordinator says so after the window ends (not yet stated for ABL-V2: the analysis stays blind until that word); no runner computes or pushes a number from this window before the reviewer's analysis. The launch of the searches is not conditioned on the reviewer, but the files above must not change after this record; a change is a new addendum before the launch of the arm it affects.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 38: advance release for ABL-V2 (2026-10-22 20:27 UTC; recorded before any ABL-V2 search has ended and before any result exists).** The coordinator released the unblinding of ABL-V2 in advance, effective once the window has ended and the reviewer has registered its results hashes. The reviewer then runs the analysis of addendum 11 at once, as fixed there: per arm, the per-seed contrasts against the K2 champion of the same seed index (task-paired, artifact-level CR1 CI, raw p), the 2-seed pooled difference against K2 s0 and s1, the sensitivity against all four K2 seeds, each arm's delta over the in-window seed, the truncation counts and lint rejections per run, the 5I drift check (block-adjusted deltas beside the plain ones when it flags), the seed-check minus seed and infrastructure counts; exploratory, raw p, no family claim, no Holm decision, no pipeline-level interval as a claim. Condition set by the coordinator: runners compute and push no ABL-V2 number before the reviewer's analysis. The release does not waive the checks before the window of addendum 11 (12J grep of all 12 runs, the NC isolation grep, the surface and lint audit of every champion, the feedback-content grep for A3a and A3b, the order review and the ack).

The reviewer did not read the contents of the NC2 scripts nor verify the sandbox claims. Searches are running; no score exists.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 12: ABL-K2-MAIN becomes one same-window table; truncation exclusion rule for the primary (2026-10-22 22:43 UTC; user decisions relayed by team-lead; registered before the ABL-K2-MAIN order is built and before any held-out score of GATE-K2 seed 1, 2 or 3 exists; amends addenda 8 and 9).** (1) *Exclusion rule, search-side only, fixed now.* A GATE-K2 seed is excluded from the PRIMARY estimate if its optimizer sessions were cut by the turn cap (last assistant `stop_reason` not `end_turn`, `optimizer_max_turns` 80, the transcript method of erratum 37) in MORE THAN 50% of its sessions. Measured counts: seed 0, 0 of 19; seed 1, 0 of 19; seed 2, 17 of 19 (89%), so seed 2 is EXCLUDED from the primary; seed 3 is measured when its search ends and the rule applies to it as written (earlier count 2 of 13; it had 17 transcripts and no `run_summary.json` at the time of this record). The rule uses no held-out score, and the measurement for seed 3 is taken, and written to the registry, before its champion is scored. The primary is therefore GATE-K2 over the included seeds (expected s0, s1, s3) against the in-window seed. This supersedes addendum 9 (4) ('no seed may be dropped') for the PRIMARY ONLY: seed 2 is still scored in the window and shown in every table, and the FULL four-seed estimate is a REGISTERED SENSITIVITY printed beside the primary. *Disclosure of reason and timing (required):* the rule was written after the truncation counts of seeds 0 to 2 and the stall of seed 2's search (best search-split score 0.733 against 0.913 and 0.927) were seen, but before any held-out score of seeds 1 to 3; the threshold of 50% separates the observed counts (0, 0, 17 of 19) at any value between 11% and 89%, so the choice of 50% decides nothing for seeds 0 to 2; the rule removes the seed whose search was weakest, which is the direction in which an exclusion can flatter the primary, so the four-seed sensitivity is part of the registered result and, if the primary and the four-seed estimate disagree on whether the 95% CI excludes 0, the headline sentence says so and neither is called the result alone. If seed 3 is excluded as well, the primary has two seeds, its pipeline-level interval (1 df) is printed only as a labelled descriptor, and this is stated. (2) *One window for every method.* ABL-K2-MAIN is expanded so that Table 2 of the paper is a single same-window table. Cells (every champion at k = 10 on test-20, adapter `9c797d8a...`, gpt-oss-120b via :4113): the four GATE-K2 champions (s0 to s3, s0 the artifact of ABL-W1); the eight published optimizers EvoSkill, GEPA, full-GEPA, Meta-Harness, MIPROv2, SkillOpt, Trace2Skill and WikiSkill, three seeds each (the main-window frozen artifacts, same tree hashes); ACE, three seeds (the LATE-window artifacts); HarnessLens: the three seeds are ONE artifact (hl-v0), so ONE cell, scored once at n = 20 trials per task through the pinned tau_v3 entrypoint as in the LATE window and standing for the three seeds (artifact-level interval only, as in addendum 3, with the disclosures printed there); NC-LOOP, one champion (the ABL-W2 artifact, tree `0c599e18`); the seed at n = 20; the seed-check at k = 10. Jobs: 4 x 200 + 24 x 200 + 3 x 200 (ACE) + 400 (hl-v0) + 200 (NC-LOOP) + 400 + 200 = 7,400. The HC-stock k = 1 champions are DROPPED from this window (k = 1 is out of the paper; the secondary contrast and the template-confound labelling of erratum 35 for K2 versus HC-stock therefore lapse, erratum 35 itself stands for the ablation windows). CapEvolve-Par is removed from the paper and is not scored. (3) *Families.* PRIMARY, m = 1: GATE-K2 (included seeds) versus the in-window seed, artifact-level CR1 t(19) CI as the test and the pipeline-level t(S - 1) interval beside it (S = number of included seeds). SECONDARY family, own Holm, m = 11 rows: GATE-K2 (included seeds) versus each of the eleven comparators (the eight published optimizers, ACE, HarnessLens hl-v0, NC-LOOP), paired by task inside the window, artifact-level CR1 test; for comparators with three seeds the pipeline-level interval is printed beside, for hl-v0 and NC-LOOP (one artifact each) artifact level only. The family is therefore m = 11 and the Holm adjusted p is the verdict of a secondary row; the raw p and the full list of rows are printed. Planning MDE (conditional, 80% power, within-variance 0.1236, tau 0.15, T = 20, k = 10; K2 with 3 included seeds against a comparator cell): 0.122 for one contrast and 0.168 at Holm m = 11; with only 2 included seeds 0.132 and 0.182; the pipeline-level intervals are wide (K2 against the seed at m = 1 with 3 seeds: 0.234; at m = 11: 0.69), so no pipeline-level claim is made for the secondary family. (4) *Sensitivities, registered:* (a) all four K2 seeds; (b) the fresh seeds only (s1 to s3, no selection on a held-out result, with and without seed 2); (c) the 5I drift rule on the in-window seed with block-adjusted deltas beside the plain ones. (5) *Disclosures that stay:* GATE-K2 was chosen as the configuration to confirm after the held-out result of its seed 0 was seen among 12 ablation arms (addendum 8); the competitor and comparator results of the earlier windows had been seen when this design was written; K2 versus a competitor compares whole methods (searches, templates, code versions, budgets, optimizer protocols differ), so a K2-versus-competitor difference is not an effect of any component; HarnessLens and NC-LOOP are single artifacts; the budgets of the comparators are those of their own V3 B1 searches, printed with the rows. (6) *Process unchanged:* the 12J and audit checks, the order review and the ack by the reviewer before launch; the analysis is pre-released on the results hashes (addendum 8); no runner computes a number from the window first.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 13: the primary excludes GATE-K2 seed 2 by name; seed 3 is included whatever its truncation (2026-10-22 22:46 UTC; user decision relayed by team-lead; registered before any held-out score of GATE-K2 seeds 1 to 3 exists; replaces addendum 12 (1), everything else in addendum 12 stands).** (1) *Rule.* The general rule 'excluded if more than 50% of the sessions were truncated' of addendum 12 (1) is REPLACED by a specific exclusion: GATE-K2 seed 2 is excluded from the PRIMARY estimate because of the search-side fault recorded in erratum 37 (the optimizer was cut at the 80-turn cap in 17 of 19 sessions, which the reviewer recounted independently). The PRIMARY is GATE-K2 seeds 0, 1 and 3 against the in-window seed. Seed 3 is INCLUDED regardless of its final truncation count (its final count is measured and printed, and the primary does not depend on it). Seed 2 is still scored in the window and shown in every table. (2) *The four-seed estimate is a REGISTERED SENSITIVITY:* it is computed and written to the registered analysis file whatever the primary says. Whether the paper prints it is the user's decision, stated by the coordinator; the reviewer's position, recorded here so that it is on file: if the primary and the four-seed estimate disagree on whether the 95% CI excludes 0, leaving the four-seed estimate out of the paper while keeping the exclusion would be selective reporting, and in that case the paper must at least state in the text that seed 2 was excluded, why, and that an estimate including it exists in the registered analysis and differs; if they agree, a one-line statement of the exclusion suffices. (3) *Disclosure of reason and timing (required):* the exclusion was decided after the truncation counts of seeds 0 to 2 and the stall of seed 2's search (best search-split score 0.733 against 0.913 and 0.927) were known, and before any held-out score of seeds 1 to 3; it removes the seed whose search was weakest, the direction in which an exclusion can flatter the primary; it names a seed instead of a threshold, so unlike addendum 12 (1) it is a decision about one seed and not a rule that would have been applied to other seeds; the search-side fault it cites is real and was measured (erratum 37), but it is a mechanism that was observed together with a poor search outcome, and the table does not show that truncation caused the poor outcome. (4) *Consequences for the pipeline-level interval:* the primary has S = 3 seeds (t with 2 df); the four-seed sensitivity has S = 4 (t with 3 df); the fresh-seeds sensitivity of addendum 12 (4) is unchanged (s1 to s3, with and without s2). (5) All other provisions of addendum 12 (one window for all methods, the 11-comparator secondary family with its own Holm, the cells, the planning MDEs for 3 included seeds, HC-stock k = 1 dropped, CapEvolve-Par not scored) and of addenda 8 to 10 and errata 35 and 37 stand.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 14: ABL-V2 reduced to one seed per arm (amends addendum 11; 2026-10-22 23:07 UTC; user decision of 2026-10-22 about 23:00 UTC relayed by team-lead; before any ABL-V2 held-out score exists).** (1) *Change.* ABL-V2 keeps ONE seed per arm, seed 0. The six seed-1 searches (A2a, A2b, A3a and A3b seed 1, and A1a and A1b seed 1) are STOPPED mid-search (reported progress 450 to 1,200 of 3,000 counted rollouts; none has a `run_summary.json`, as the reviewer checked at 23:07 UTC). The stopped seed-1 runs are NOT scored, are not replaced and no artifact of them enters any window; their partial logs are kept and may be cited as 'stopped' only. (2) *Contrasts.* Per arm: the arm champion of seed 0 against the K2 champion of seed 0 (task-paired, artifact-level CR1 CI, raw p), and the arm champion against the in-window seed. The pooled 2-seed contrasts of addendum 11 (1) (b) and (c), and the pipeline-level descriptor, are WITHDRAWN: with one seed per arm there is no pooled contrast and no pipeline-level interval, and no sentence about an arm is a statement about the method, only about one searched artifact. Planning MDE for the single-champion contrasts (conditional, 80% power, within-variance 0.1236, tau 0.15, T = 20, k = 10, uncorrected): 0.158 against a single K2 champion, 0.118 to 0.122 against a seed cell or a pooled K2; differences below these are inconclusive; with ten arm contrasts, one or two raw p-values under 0.05 are expected by chance. The K2 reference of the arm contrast is K2 seed 0 (the artifact of ABL-W1, which was scored there, and re-scored in the window); K2 seeds 1 and 3 enter the window as cells of ABL-K2-MAIN (addendum 12) and may be shown as additional references, with no pairing by seed index. (3) *Window.* The ABL-V2 cells become: 6 arm champions (seed 0), K2 seed 0 (the reference; it is also a cell of ABL-K2-MAIN, and the reviewer will rule on whether ABL-V2 and ABL-K2-MAIN can be one window when the orders arrive: the same in-window seed and K2 s0 would serve both, which removes a cross-window comparison, at the price of a larger window; the decision and its reason are recorded at the order review), the seed at n = 20, the seed-check at k = 10, all at k = 10 on test-20, adapter `9c797d8a...`. (4) *Disclosure of reason and timing (required):* the reduction is for budget and time; it was decided before any held-out result of any ABL-V2 arm exists, and before any search-split result of the arms was seen by the reviewer. All other provisions of addendum 11 (arms and surface paragraphs, lint, truncation audit per run, champion rule, checks before the window, the advance release of erratum 38 and the rule that runners compute no number first) and erratum 39 stand. Retained as disclosures: arms designed after the K2 and V3 B1 results; one seed per arm; A2a stricter than K2 on docstrings; A1a and A1b differ from K2 in more than one way.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 15: ABL-V2 and ABL-K2-MAIN merge into one window, FINAL-MERGED (2026-10-22 23:19 UTC; user decision relayed by team-lead; before any held-out score of the merged window exists; amends addenda 11, 12, 13 and 14).** (1) *Merge.* ABL-V2 (reduced to seed 0 by addendum 14) and ABL-K2-MAIN (addenda 8, 9, 12, 13) become ONE window, run after the six ABL-V2 seed-0 searches have ended (expected about 16:00 UTC 2026-10-23, as stated by team-lead). One in-window seed (n = 20 trials per task) and one seed-check (k = 10) serve every contrast, so no arm-versus-K2 or K2-versus-competitor contrast crosses windows. *Timing and reason:* the merge is decided before the ABL-V2 champions exist and before any held-out score of any cell of the merged window exists (the held-out scores that exist for some cells come from earlier windows, are disclosed in the addenda concerned, and are re-scored here). The reviewer had recommended merging (addendum 14 note); the user decided. (2) *Cells (every champion at k = 10 on test-20, adapter `9c797d8a...`, gpt-oss-120b via :4113):* GATE-K2 s0 to s3 (4); the eight published optimizers EvoSkill, GEPA, full-GEPA, Meta-Harness, MIPROv2, SkillOpt, Trace2Skill and WikiSkill x 3 seeds (24; main-window artifacts); ACE x 3 (LATE-window artifacts); hl-v0 once at n = 20 (standing for the three HarnessLens seeds); NC-LOOP (1; the ABL-W2 artifact); the six ABL-V2 seed-0 champions (A1a, A1b, A2a, A2b, A3a, A3b; the scored artifact of an NC arm is its `/work/champion` tree); the seed at n = 20; the seed-check at k = 10. Jobs: 4 x 200 + 24 x 200 + 3 x 200 + 400 + 200 + 6 x 200 + 400 + 200 = 8,600, as announced. (3) *Families, unchanged and all in the same window:* (a) PRIMARY, m = 1: GATE-K2 seeds 0, 1 and 3 against the seed (addendum 13), artifact-level CR1 t(19), pipeline-level t(2) beside it; the four-seed estimate is the registered sensitivity of addendum 13; the fresh-seed sensitivity of addendum 12 (4) stands. (b) SECONDARY, own Holm, m = 11: GATE-K2 (seeds 0, 1, 3) against each of the eleven comparators (the 8 optimizers, ACE, hl-v0, NC-LOOP), paired by task (addendum 12 (3)); planning MDE 0.122 for one contrast and 0.168 at m = 11. (c) ABLATION contrasts, exploratory, raw p, no family claim, no Holm decision, no pipeline-level interval: for each of the six arms, the seed-0 champion against K2 seed 0 (task-paired, artifact-level CR1) and against the in-window seed (addendum 14); planning MDE 0.158 for a single champion against a single champion; K2 s0 is the artifact scored in ABL-W1 and re-scored here. The three families are reported in separate tables with their own labels; no row belongs to two families except the K2 s0 reference cell, which is a cell, not a contrast. (4) *Drift and ordering:* the merged window has 8,600 jobs at 8 workers concurrently with the ABL-V2 searches (or alone, if they have ended); the seed and the seed-check trials must be interleaved over the whole order (the reviewer checks their positions at the order review); the 5I drift rule applies, with block-adjusted deltas beside the plain ones when it flags, and if the block-adjusted and plain verdicts of the primary disagree both are reported. (5) *Process:* the single order, manifest, champions file and scorer pins come to the reviewer after the six ABL-V2 searches end and their checks of addendum 11 (12J grep of the six runs, NC isolation grep, surface and lint audit of every champion, feedback-content grep for A3a and A3b); the reviewer reviews, registers and acks the single order; the advance release of the analysis (addendum 8, erratum 38) applies on the results hashes; no runner computes or pushes a number from the window first. The earlier separate orders ABL-K2-MAIN and ABL-V2 are not built.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 49: the MERGED analysis script is registered before the data exist (2026-10-23 13:49 UTC; no result exists).** `analysis/scripts/merged_analysis.py` implements addenda 12, 13, 14, 15 and errata 41, 42, 48 and was tested only on two SYNTHETIC stand-ins of the results files (random Bernoulli trials with a known shift; no real data; the files were in `/tmp` and are not part of the record). The tests found and fixed two defects before any real use: a key-format error in the comparator cell names, and the omission of the 5I rule that blocks holding fewer than 5% of the in-window seed trials are merged into the neighbouring block (without it a final block of five trials made the drift check flag spuriously). What the script does, fixed now: PRIMARY K2 seeds 0, 1, 3 against the in-window seed (artifact-level CR1 t(19), pipeline-level t(S - 1)); SENSITIVITIES always computed: seeds 0 to 3, seeds 0 and 1, seeds 1 to 3, seeds 1 and 3; SECONDARY K2 (0, 1, 3) against each of eleven comparators with its own Holm (m = 11); SIX ablation contrasts (each ABL-V2 seed-0 champion against K2 seed 0 and against the in-window seed, raw p); the drift check on hour blocks with the small-block merge, block-adjusted deltas beside the plain ones when it flags (more than 2 SE); the seed-check minus seed; infrastructure counts; and a FLAGS line that lists every sensitivity whose 95% CI disagrees with the primary on exclusion of 0 (plain, and block-adjusted when the drift flags). Pipeline-level intervals are printed for the primary, the K2 sensitivities and the 3-seed comparators only. The block-adjustment is applied to every cell with the in-window seed's block effects, as in the earlier windows. If the real data expose a further defect, it is fixed by an erratum that states what changed and why; the registered specification above does not change.

The seed, K = 1 and K = 2 were scored in ONE final window: `test_normal`, 168 tasks, 2 trials, 1,008 cells in one shuffled order. Results as posted: seed TGC 37.8 / SGC 15.2; K = 1 51.2 / 28.6 (gain +13.4 [7.1, 20.2] TGC); K = 2 47.3 / 27.7 (gain +9.5 [2.1, 17.3] TGC); K = 2 minus K = 1 -3.9 [-11.6, 3.9] TGC; CIs are scenario-clustered paired bootstrap (56 clusters), unadjusted. In each arm 1 of 6 iterations was accepted (iteration 1). *No pre-registration.* The registry holds no line for AppWorld, for these two arms or for a choice between them; the issues are plans for both arms, written before the run, that do not say which would be reported. The registered stock gate is `k_se = 1` (plan 7A, 8F, 9G, and the AppWorld design `designs/appworld_experimental_design.md`, `hcF`); `k = 2` entered this plan as the tau2 confirmation setting of addendum 8, chosen after a held-out look (addendum 8 disclosure). Therefore the choice of K = 2 for the paper's AppWorld row is NOT justified by pre-registration. *Reason for reporting K = 2 (user decision, relayed by team-lead):* consistency with the tau2 headline configuration, which is k = 2. K = 1 scored higher in this window (51.2 against 47.3), so the choice of the lower arm is conservative, and it is made on a consistency argument and not on the outcome. *Reporting rules fixed here:* (1) the paper shows the K = 1 row in the appendix beside K = 2, with K2 minus K1 -3.9 [-11.6, 3.9]; (2) the AppWorld K = 2 row is compared with its own seed in its own window; competitors scored in other windows are compared only through the gain over each window's own baseline (batch baselines 34.2 and 33.3 against 37.8 here), never by raw TGC across windows; (3) the AppWorld rows carry the same fit-metric footnote as the earlier CapEvolve code path (train and dev used together for search), one search seed, one accepted iteration of six, and the unadjusted CI; (4) the pipeline-level claim is not available (one search seed per arm); (5) the isolation claims of #910 (a sandbox with 21 planted secrets, 0 leaked) are the executing agent's report and are not verified by the reviewer.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 54: correction of the multiplicity wording in errata 52 and in earlier messages (2026-10-24 11:06 UTC; wording only, no number changes).** Several of the reviewer's texts (erratum 52, the MERGED report, messages to the coordinator and to mlsys-lead) say that 'one or two raw p-values below 0.05 are expected by chance' among the six ablation contrasts. That is overstated. If no arm differed from the reference and the six contrasts were independent, the expected number of raw p-values below 0.05 is 0.3, the probability of at least one is 0.27 and of at least two 0.03. The six contrasts are not independent: all share K2 seed 0 as the reference, so a reference that is high or low by chance moves every contrast the same way (five of the six differences from K2 seed 0 are near -0.09). K2 seed 0 (0.630) is not an outlier among the K2 seeds scored in the same window (0.645, 0.565, 0.625), which limits but does not remove that concern. The correct sentence is: 'with six registered contrasts that share one reference artifact, a raw p below 0.05 is not unlikely even when no arm differs (about one in four if they were independent); the p-values are a rough guide and are not corrected'. The registered numbers and the analysis file are unchanged.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 55: the paper presents the ablation study as five arms and does not mention a sixth registered contrast (user decision, taken knowing that it departs from the registry; relayed by team-lead; 2026-10-24 20:35 UTC; reporting choice only, no number changes).** The registry holds six ablation contrasts (addenda 14 and 15, erratum 47: the six ABL-V2 seed-0 champions against K2 seed 0 and against the seed). The user decided that the paper presents five ablations and says nothing about a sixth registered contrast. This SUPERSEDES the reporting rule of erratum 52 (2) and (4), which required that the count of six registered contrasts be recoverable and that the multiplicity sentence say six; the registry, this plan and the reviewer's analysis file (`merged.md`) still hold all six, A1b unchanged. The reviewer records the following consequences, which are the reviewer's, and which the user decided against: (1) the paper's description of the ablation study then differs from the registered design in the number of contrasts, so a reader of the registry will find six where the paper shows five; the five-contrast multiplicity sentence below is correct for the five shown but it is not the registered family size; (2) the dropped arm is not described, so the paper gives no signal that an arm was run and not shown; (3) the plain raw p-value that the dropped arm had against K2 seed 0 (0.040, block-adjusted 0.054) is not reported. The reviewer does not regard this as selective reporting of a favourable result (the dropped arm was one of the two with a raw p below 0.05, and its removal makes the remaining set look LESS significant, not more), but it is an omission of a registered contrast and the reviewer's disclosure that it is one is on file here. *The sentence for five contrasts that share one reference (search 1):* 'Five contrasts with search 1 are shown and they share that one reference, so they are correlated. If no arm differed from search 1 and the contrasts were independent, we would expect 0.25 raw p-values below 0.05, and the chance of at least one would be 0.23 (of at least two, 0.02); the shared reference makes these figures rough. The p-values are raw and uncorrected.' The computation is 5 x 0.05 = 0.25, 1 - 0.95^5 = 0.226, and 1 - 0.95^5 - 5 x 0.05 x 0.95^4 = 0.023.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ADDENDUM 18: the TRAINRS1 window and the transfer-ratio analysis (2026-10-25; descriptive; registered before any TRAINRS1 score exists; requested by team-lead for the paper's transfer figure).** *Purpose.* The paper's 0.28 (pooled held-out gain over search re-score gain, 8 published optimizers) mixes search re-scores from several sessions, and CapEvolve's 0.41 uses its in-sample acceptance score, which is a different quantity; reviewers have flagged this. TRAINRS1 re-scores every arm on the 30 TRAIN ids at k = 5 in ONE session, so one definition of the search gain is used for every arm. *Cells (ablation-lead's dry build; the order, manifest and champions file come to the reviewer for hashing and an ack, and the cell list below is checked against them):* 32 cells at 150 jobs each on train-30 (4,800 jobs, no test id): CapEvolve searches 1, 2, 3 (K2 s0 `cand_0014`, s1 `cand_0013`, s3 `cand_0011`; s2 excluded by name); the MERGED champions of the 8 published optimizers (gepafull, gepa, skillopt, wikiskill, metaharness, evoskill, mipro, trace2skill) x 3 seeds; the seed at n = 20 (600 jobs); a seed-check at k = 5. *Estimator (fixed now).* The numerator G_test is taken from the MERGED window, which is already scored and seen (each arm's mean delta over the MERGED seed, averaged over an arm's seeds); the only new quantity is the denominator G_search = the arm's TRAINRS1 train-30 mean minus the TRAINRS1 seed mean. gamma = G_test / G_search. The pooled published gamma is the ratio of the sums over the eight arms, the same estimator as `pooled_ratio()` in the paper's `mw_fig_data.py` (registered by hash as the reference); CapEvolve's gamma is the ratio of the sums over searches 1, 2, 3 with per-search values beside. Analysis script: `trainrs1_analysis.py` (tested on a synthetic stand-in only), bootstrap over test tasks and train tasks, and over the eight arms for the pooled value; an arms-only bootstrap is printed beside for comparison with the earlier interval. The seed-check minus seed difference on train-30 is printed as the session's noise check. *Interpretation fixed now.* (a) The train ids are the search data of every arm: the re-score is IN-SAMPLE and labelled fit-set; gamma measures the train-to-test gap and not generalisation to new tasks. (b) Fresh trials remove noise-selection but not task-selection (winner's curse remains). (c) CapEvolve's acceptance rule selects on these 30 tasks under a gate, so its fresh G_search can fall more than the published arms', which raises its gamma; the paper must say that a higher gamma for CapEvolve is not by itself evidence of better generalisation if its G_search is smaller. (d) The CapEvolve minus published difference of gammas is DESCRIPTIVE: three searches against eight arms, one search seed each for the unit of CapEvolve; no pipeline-level interval and no test. (e) The earlier 0.28 and 0.41 are not changed in the record; the paper should replace them by the TRAINRS1 values and say so, and must not mix the two. (f) The numerators were seen before the denominators exist, and the reviewer fixes that here so that no ratio can be chosen after the fact. (g) Release: the window result is released for analysis by the advance release stated in addendum 17; the runners compute and push nothing before the reviewer's run.

Checks: 4,500 jobs, all split train over the same 30 ids, 0 test ids, `final_split` false, no canary; cells = seed 300 (k = 10), seed-check 150 (k = 5), GATE-K2-search1/2/3 150 each, the eight published arms x 3 seeds at 150 each; adapter `9c797d8a`; all 27 artifact trees equal the MERGED trees (compared one by one), none inert. The builder differs from `v3_order.py` in three lines (seed-check kept in the train block; seed trials = `--seed-trials`; tag `FINAL-E-TRAINRS1`), read in a diff. The team-lead's later note asking to accept the 4,800 build is superseded by ablation-lead's rebuild to the list in addendum 18. (2) *Corrections to addendum 18.* (a) The estimator is the one that reproduced the paper's numbers (pooled gamma = sum of held-out gains over sum of search gains, 8 arm means; arm bootstrap of 10,000 resamples, `random.seed(0)`, 2.5 and 97.5 percentiles; CapEvolve = ratio of means over its three searches), implemented in `trainrs1_gamma.py`, registered before the data exist; `trainrs1_analysis.py` (registered earlier) is SUPERSEDED and not to be run. (b) The eight published arms are evoskill, gepa, gepafull, metaharness, mipro, skillopt, trace2skill and wikiskill (not ao22, hc3 or hcstock). (c) The numerators are the MERGED held-out gains over the MERGED in-window seed (0.500) for ALL arms, CapEvolve and published; they are fixed and visible. **5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 65: user reporting decisions of 2026-10-24, relayed by team-lead on 2026-10-25 (logged as the user's decisions; no registry number changes; the reviewer does not block on them).** The user was given the reviewer's rules R1 to R7 (erratum 57) and the risk, and decided: (a) AppWorld reports ONLY the k = 0.2 run; the k = 1 and k = 2 runs are not mentioned anywhere and the run-after-seen and best-of-three disclosure is removed (this withdraws R1 and R3 of erratum 57 and the part of R2 that names the other windows; R2's cross-window label and R4 to R7 are not named in the decision and remain the reviewer's recommendation, to be answered by team-lead); (b) the one-seed and exploratory caveats are removed from the paper, and the number of searches is stated once as a fact in Setup; (c) the SpreadsheetBench results of #538 and the appendix move to the main text, with competitor numbers labelled as reported; (d) no statement about AI use in preparing the paper. *Reviewer's record of the risk, for the file:* under (a) the AppWorld headline is the best of three gate settings run after the other two were scored and the paper will not say so; the TGC 61.6 against 37.8 is also a cross-window comparison. A reader cannot judge selection or drift from the paper. Under (b) the tau2 interval for the pipeline includes zero and the abstract-level claim is about three searches; stating the number of searches once keeps the quantity identified but drops the statement that the evidence is exploratory. The decisions are the user's. *Corrections of fact to the edit-surface descriptions, to be applied in the paper (team-lead):* Meta-Harness edits tool documentation in 1 of 3 seeds (seed 1 only); HarnessLens proposed tool patches but accepted none, and its champion is the unmodified seed. *SpreadsheetBench main-text numbers checked against #538 (scoreboard comment 6264957598) and the GPT-5.5 comment 5937950019, from the issue text, not recomputed from rollouts:* Opus 5 optimizer 76.8 (runs 75.4, 77.1, 77.9; per-run lower bounds 69.6, 71.6, 72.8); sealed Gemma 71.2 (72.5, 71.2, 69.9; spread 2.6; interval over the three runs [68.0, 74.4]); WikiSkill 68.0; SkillOpt 63.1; no-skill 43.5 (ours) against 48.3 (theirs); gains +33.3 against 43.5 (+33.0 as the mean paired gain on the 276 paired tasks) and +27.7; GPT-5.5 87.6 +- 0.6 (88.2, 87.5, 87.1), gain +49.1 +- 0.5, against SkillOpt direct chat 80.7 from 41.8. *Rules for the main text:* (SB1) the sealed-harness Gemma row (71.2) must NOT be described as above 68.0: its interval over the three runs is [68.0, 74.4], so it touches WikiSkill's reported figure; only the Opus 5 row has every run's lower bound above 68.0, and that bound is the measurement interval over 280 tasks for one run, not a pipeline interval; (SB2) gains are measured from our own no-skill floor (43.5), which is below the competitors' reported 48.3, so a gain-to-gain comparison is against a different start and the text must say so in the same sentence; (SB3) competitor numbers are labelled as reported, not run by us, with unstated turn budget, decoding and tools; the optimizer differs (Claude Opus 5, or Gemma for the sealed row); (SB4) the test split is development-exposed and the \cs{} runs used earlier code versions that predate the gold-answer isolation, not verified isolated (the appendix text says so; the main text must not drop it); (SB5) one trial per task and three runs per row, so the spread is across runs; (SB6) the +33.3 and the +33.0 are different quantities (test minus the no-skill score against the mean paired gain on 276 tasks) and must not be mixed; (SB7) GPT-5.5 against SkillOpt's 80.7 is against its direct-chat setting only, with a different harness, and the 41.8 and 38.6 starts differ; (SB8) the controlled 70-task comparison remains the only one in which every optimizer was run by us and is the one that shows the k = 1 arm at +0.105 against +0.110 to +0.152 for eight others; if the main text shows the case-study table it must not omit that. The reviewer has not yet seen the new main-text draft and will review it against this erratum and `app_sb.tex` before merge.

**5-AMENDMENT 12 ERRATUM 66: further user decisions of 2026-10-25 on disclosures, relayed by team-lead (logged as the user's decisions; no registry number changes; the reviewer does not block on them).** The user, with SB1 to SB8 (erratum 65) and the AppWorld cross-window point laid out, decided to drop from BOTH the main text and the appendix: (i) the AppWorld cross-session (cross-window) label; (ii) the SpreadsheetBench note that the runs predate the gold-answer isolation and that the test split is development-exposed (SB4); (iii) the controlled 70-task SpreadsheetBench comparison (SB8). (ii) With the isolation and exposure note removed, the SpreadsheetBench case-study numbers are presented without the facts that the runs are not verified as isolated from gold answers and that the test split was used during development; both bear on how far the test scores can be trusted. (iii) With the controlled comparison removed, the only SpreadsheetBench comparison in which every optimizer was run by us, in which CapEvolve's k = 1 arm gained +0.105 against +0.110 to +0.152 for eight others, is absent, and the remaining comparison is against competitor figures as reported; the paper's SpreadsheetBench claim therefore rests on an unmatched comparison only. These are the user's decisions; the reviewer's recommendation to keep (i) to (iii) stands on the record. The reviewer will check the SB and AppWorld numbers on `origin/mlsys-lead/rewrite` when mlsys-lead pushes the next batch.

Analysis by the registered `bridge1_analysis.py`, unchanged: means NC-1SHOT 0.610, GATE-K2-s0 (K2 search 1) 0.585, seed 0.472 (n = 20), seed-check 0.450 (k = 10). NC-1SHOT minus K2 search 1, task-paired: +0.025, 95% CI [-0.099, 0.149], raw p 0.678 (PRIMARY, exploratory; below the detectable difference of 0.158 for a single champion against another, so inconclusive). NC-1SHOT minus seed: +0.138 [0.015, 0.260], raw p 0.030. K2 search 1 minus seed (window check; earlier windows +0.145, +0.130, +0.130): +0.113 [-0.006, 0.231], raw p 0.062. NC-1SHOT's own gain over its seed is +0.138 here against +0.040 in ABL-W2 for the same artifact (tree `ce6b5eeb`); both are reported. One champion per arm: no pipeline-level interval and no statement about Claude Code or CapEvolve in general. NC-1SHOT never ran its own edit, exposes 19 tools against 14, and is a single Claude Code session. Raw p, exploratory, not in any Holm family.
